{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016m4mnGJM7eQGXKDivdhqB5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01REz6UthymNWUTC1P54LaYt","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"cqa1-33015","command":"cat > /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/test_pi05_quick.py << 'PYEOF'\n\"\"\"Quick diagnostic test: pi05 on LIBERO-Object, 2 episodes, verbose output.\"\"\"\nimport os, sys, time, types, logging\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom collections import deque\n\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\nos.environ[\"HF_HOME\"] = \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch._dynamo; torch._dynamo.config.disable = True\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, force=True, format=\"%(asctime)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nDEVICE = \"cuda:0\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/pi05_libero_finetuned\"\n\nlog.info(\"Loading model...\")\nfrom lerobot.policies.pi05.modeling_pi05 import PI05Policy\npolicy = PI05Policy.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n\n# DO NOT PATCH embed_image - use default\npolicy = policy.to(DEVICE)\npolicy.eval()\nlog.info(f\"Model: {sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters())/1e6:.1f}M params\")\n\nfrom lerobot.policies.factory import make_pre_post_processors\npreprocess, postprocess = make_pre_post_processors(\n    policy.config, MODEL_PATH,\n    preprocessor_overrides={\"device_processor\": {\"device\": str(DEVICE)}},\n)\nlog.info(\"Preprocessor/postprocessor loaded\")\n\nfrom libero.libero import benchmark, get_libero_path\nfrom libero.libero.envs import OffScreenRenderEnv\n\nnp.random.seed(42)\ntorch.manual_seed(42)\ntask_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark_dict()[\"libero_object\"]()\n\ndef quat_to_axisangle(quat):\n    q = quat.copy()\n    if q[3] > 1.0: q[3] = 1.0\n    elif q[3] < -1.0: q[3] = -1.0\n    den = np.sqrt(1.0 - q[3]**2)\n    if abs(den) < 1e-8:\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\n    return (q[:3] * 2.0 * np.arccos(q[3]) / den).astype(np.float32)\n\ntask = task_suite.get_task(0)\ninit_states = task_suite.get_task_init_states(0)\nbddl = Path(get_libero_path(\"bddl_files\")) / task.problem_folder / task.bddl_file\nenv = OffScreenRenderEnv(bddl_file_name=str(bddl), camera_heights=256, camera_widths=256)\nenv.seed(42)\ntask_desc = task.language\nlog.info(f\"Task: {task_desc}\")\n\nfor ep in range(2):\n    env.reset()\n    obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[ep])\n    for _ in range(10):\n        obs, _, _, _ = env.step([0.0]*6 + [-1.0])\n    \n    action_queue = deque()\n    max_reward = -999\n    success = False\n    \n    for t in range(280):\n        if not action_queue:\n            base_img = obs[\"agentview_image\"]\n            wrist_img = obs[\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]\n            base_t = torch.from_numpy(base_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n            base_t = torch.flip(base_t, dims=[1, 2])\n            wrist_t = torch.from_numpy(wrist_img.copy()).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\n            wrist_t = torch.flip(wrist_t, dims=[1, 2])\n            \n            eef_pos = obs[\"robot0_eef_pos\"]\n            eef_quat = obs[\"robot0_eef_quat\"]\n            gripper_qpos = obs[\"robot0_gripper_qpos\"]\n            state = np.concatenate([\n                eef_pos, quat_to_axisangle(eef_quat), gripper_qpos\n            ]).astype(np.float32)\n            state_t = torch.from_numpy(state)\n            \n            raw_obs = {\n                \"observation.images.image\": base_t,\n                \"observation.images.image2\": wrist_t,\n                \"observation.state\": state_t,\n                \"task\": task_desc,\n            }\n            batch = preprocess(raw_obs)\n            \n            with torch.no_grad():\n                actions = policy.predict_action_chunk(batch)\n            \n            actions_unnorm = postprocess(actions)\n            action_np = actions_unnorm[0].cpu().numpy()\n            \n            if t == 0:\n                log.info(f\"  Ep{ep} first action chunk shape: {action_np.shape}\")\n                log.info(f\"  Ep{ep} first action: {action_np[0]}\")\n                log.info(f\"  Ep{ep} action range: [{action_np.min():.4f}, {action_np.max():.4f}]\")\n            \n            for a in action_np[:5]:\n                action_queue.append(a[:7])\n        \n        action = action_queue.popleft()\n        obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action.tolist())\n        \n        if reward > max_reward:\n            max_reward = reward\n        \n        if t < 5 or t % 50 == 0:\n            log.info(f\"  Ep{ep} t={t}: reward={reward:.4f}, done={done}, info_keys={list(info.keys())}\")\n        \n        if done or reward > 0.5:\n            success = True\n            log.info(f\"  Ep{ep} SUCCESS at t={t}! reward={reward}, done={done}\")\n            break\n    \n    log.info(f\"  Ep{ep} final: success={success}, max_reward={max_reward:.4f}, steps={t+1}\")\n\nenv.close()\nlog.info(\"Done\")\nPYEOF\necho \"Script created\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1647,"cache_read_input_tokens":140654,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1647},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6d82359-e118-47ba-aeae-b71fe82fa077","uuid":"c5fcda6c-6ee7-4c05-99fd-aff528b80a45"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016WV7AeiXBNCJWuv9qDgLyv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"The output is not updating. The model loading is taking a very long time. Let me check the process state more carefully.\n\nActually, `cat /proc/pid/fd/1` reads from the file descriptor, which points to the same file as the log. Since the process has the file open and is writing to it through the OS buffer, the `cat` reads the actual file content from disk, not the in-memory buffer.\n\nThe issue is that the output is in an OS/kernel write buffer that hasn't been flushed to disk yet. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01EczywNCvqrT3WSGSM8FCBz","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I can see the diagnostic output!\n\nKey observations:\n1. **Action chunk shape**: (50, 7) — correct, 50 actions with 7 DOF\n2. **First action**: `[-0.04566983 -0.03941117 -0.29583722 0.00338931 -0.02366902 0.0229435 -0.08390342]` — these are reasonable magnitudes for robot actions\n3. **Action range**: `[-1.0283, 0.3905]` — reasonable range\n\n\n4. **Reward**: `0.0000` at every step — the robot is not succeeding\n5. **`done`**: `False` at every step — confirms `done` is never True\n6. **`info_keys`**: `[]` — EMPTY info dict!\n\nThe `info` dictionary from `env.step()` is completely empty, so there's no `info['success']` key to check. The xvla script relies on `reward > 0.5` as the success signal, but our test shows `reward=0.0000` consistently, meaning the model isn't actually succeeding at the task.\n\nThe model should be working fine since it was fine-tuned on LIBERO Object with 99.4% success on seed43, but something's off. The actions look reasonable in magnitude, yet the reward stays at zero. I'm wondering if the issue is in how inputs are being preprocessed — the test script uses `make_pre_post_processors` from LeRobot, which might process images and state differently than the original manual preprocessing pipeline with its specific tokenizer and normalization. Let me trace through what `make_pre_post_processors` actually does with the input data.\n\nActually, I'm going in circles here. The old script also failed on Object, and the seed43 results that showed 99.4% used this same new script with `make_pre_post_processors`, so preprocessing probably isn't the culprit. The real question is whether the robot is actually completing the task — if `done` never becomes True and `reward` stays at zero, maybe it's just not reaching the goal within the 280-step limit. Let me look at the episode timing to see when task completion actually happens.\n\nThe logging pattern shows individual entries from t=0 to t=4, then jumps to t=50, and the script only logs when t < 5 or t % 50 == 0. If the entire episode runs to t=280 without any positive reward, the model is genuinely failing. But that doesn't make sense — the fine-tuned model achieved 99.4% success on this exact task, so something about the current evaluation setup must be different. Let me compare how the old working script and the new one handle image preprocessing.\n\nThe old script manually converts images to tensors, normalizes them to [0, 1] by dividing by 255, and flips them. The new script does the same initial processing but then passes everything through `make_pre_post_processors`, which might apply ImageNet normalization, resizing, state normalization, and text tokenization on top. If ImageNet normalization gets applied to images that are already in [0, 1] range, that's double-normalization and would completely throw off the model's expectations. The training config shows `use_imagenet_stats: true`, so the preprocessor is definitely applying those statistics.\n\nThe actions coming out look reasonable though—not near-zero or NaN—which means the model is actually receiving input and computing something. Maybe the real issue isn't the image normalization at all, but something about how the environment evaluates success. Let me check what's happening with the `info` dict in LIBERO.\n\nActually, robosuite's step function probably returns an empty `info` dict by default, so that's not necessarily a red flag. The task success gets tracked through the environment's own `check_success()` method or the reward signal. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_016EbSkiZE5oeprNodAMMMKi","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I have the full diagnostic output. Key findings:\n\n1. **No embed_image patch** (using default): Still 0% SR\n2. **reward = 0.0000** for ALL 280 steps in BOTH episodes - the reward is NEVER non-zero\n3. **done = False** always\n4. **info_keys = []** - empty info dict\n5. **Actions look reasonable**: [-0.046, -0.039, -0.296, ...] with range [-1.03, 0.39]\n\nThe fact that reward is ALWAYS 0.0000 is very suspicious. \n\nLIBERO uses sparse rewards—it stays at 0 until task completion, then jumps to 1.0. Since the model was fine-tuned on this exact task and achieved 99.4% success rate, the issue isn't the reward function itself but something about how the model is being deployed. The model is generating reasonable actions, yet it's never completing the task, which suggests a fundamental mismatch between the fine-tuning setup and the current evaluation.\n\nI'm checking several potential culprits: whether the action space scaling or unnormalization is incorrect, if the image preprocessing (like the horizontal flip operation) is being applied properly, whether the state representation matches what the model expects, and if the camera feeds are coming from the right sources. Looking at the actual action values in the test output—ranging from -1.03 to 0.39 across the sequence—these are unnormalized values, and I need to verify they're within the expected joint velocity limits for the robot.\n\nThe core issue is likely in the postprocessor: it should be reversing the MEAN_STD normalization that was applied during training, but if the normalizer statistics are mismatched or loaded incorrectly, the actions would come out scaled wrong. I'm checking the safetensors files that store these normalization parameters to see if they're consistent with what the model was trained on.\n\nLooking at the first action output, the magnitudes seem plausible—the negative z-component suggests downward movement, and the gripper value is reasonable. So the unnormalized actions might actually be correct.\n\nBut the reward staying at zero throughout the entire 280-step episode is suspicious. Either the robot isn't reaching the target location, or it's failing to grasp the object. I should check whether the action normalization stats are causing the model to output values in the wrong scale or format. %H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\\n    \\\"libero_spatial\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_object\\\": 800,\\n    \\\"libero_goal\\\": 800,\\n p.add_argument(\\\"--model_path\\\", type=str, default=\\\"2toINF/X-VLA-Libero\\\",\\n                   help=\\\"HuggingFace model path or local dir\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--suite\\\", type=str, default=\\\"libero_object\\\",\\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--keep_ratio\\\", type=float, default=1.0,\\n                   help=\\\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_episodes\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=int, default=0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_steps\\\", type=int, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--task_ids\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--domain_id\\\", type=int, default=3)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--denoise_steps\\\", type=int, default=10,\\n                   help=\\\"Flow matching denoising steps\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--mode\\\", type=str, default=\\\"l2_norm\\\",\\n                   choices=[\\\"l2_norm\\\", \\\"random\\\"],\\n ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Pruning hook\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float, mode: str = \\\"l2_norm\\\"):\\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\\n        log.info(\\\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\\\", keep_ratio)\\n        return\\n\\n    original_encode = model.vlm._encode_image\\n\\n    @wraps(original_encode)\\n    def pruned_encode(pixel_values):\\n        features = original_encode(pixel_values)\\n        N = features.shape[1]\\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\\n        if k >= N:\\n            return features\\n        if mode == \\\"l2_norm\\\":\\n            norms = features.norm(dim=-1)\\n            _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\\n        elif mode == \\\"random\\\":\\n            indices = torch.stack([\\n                torch.randperm(N, device=features.device)[:k]\\n                for _ in range(features.shape[0])\\n            ], dim=0)\\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\\n        pruned = features.gather(\\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, features.shape[-1])\\n        )\\n        return pruned\\n\\n    model.vlm._encode_image = pruned_encode\\n    log.info(\\\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f, mode=%s\\\", keep_ratio, mode)\\n\\n\\n# --------------------------------------------------------\\n\\n...                    if not images:\\n                        raise ValueError(f\\\"No images found in obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}\\\")\\n\\n                    raw_actions = predict_actions(\\n                        model, processor, images, task_desc, proprio_20d,\\n                        args.domain_id, device, args.denoise_steps,\\n                    )  # [30, 20]\\n\\n                    #     actions_7d = decode_action_20d_to_7d(raw_actions)  # [30, 7]\\n                    action_queue = list(actions_7d)\\n\\n                action = action_queue.pop(0)\\n                obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\\n\\n                if done or reward > 0.5:\\n                    success = True\\n                    break\\n\\n            ep_steps = step_idx + 1\\n            task_successes.append(success)\\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\\n            log.info(\\\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\\\",\\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\\n                     \\\"SUCCESS\\\" if success else \\\"FAIL\\\", ep_steps)\\n\\n            env.close()\\n\\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\\n        n_total = len(task_successes)\\n        sr = n_succ / n_total if n_total > 0 else 0.0\\n\\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\\n            \\\"task_id\\\": task_id,\\n            \\\"task_description\\\": task_desc,\\n            \\\"success_count\\\": n_succ,\\n            \\\"total_episodes\\\": n_total,\\n            \\\"success_rate\\\": round(sr, 4),\\n            \\\"mean_steps\\\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\\n        }\\n        total_successes += n_succ\\n        total_episodes += n_total\\n        log.info(\\\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\\\", task_id, n_succ, n_total, sr * 100)\\n\\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - eval_start\\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\\n\\n    log.info(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    log.info(\\\"SUITE: %s | MODEL: xvla | keep_ratio: %.2f\\\", args.suite, args.keep_ratio)\\n    log.info(\\\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\\\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\\n    log.info(\\\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\\\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\\n        log.info(\\\" log.info(\\\"Elapsed: %.1f min\\\", elapsed / 60)\\n    log.info(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n\\n    summary = {\\n        \\\"model\\\": \\\"xvla\\\",\\n        \\\"model_path\\\": args.model_path,\\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01TwHTDCLoCSd1xCQ59SEs9b","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"PI05Pytorch has embed_prefix\\\\n    def embed_prefix(\\\\n        self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks\\\\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Embed images with SigLIP and language tokens with embedding layer.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        embs = []\\\\n        pad_masks = []\\\\n        att_masks = []\\\\n\\\\n        # Process images\\\\n        for img, img_mask in zip(images, img_masks, strict=True):\\\\n\\\\n            def image_embed_func(img):\\\\n                return self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_image(img)\\\\n\\\\n            img_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(image_embed_func, img)\\\\n            bsize, num_img_embs = img_emb.shape[:2]\\\\n\\\\n            embs.append(img_emb)\\\\n            pad_masks.append(img_mask[:, None].expand(bsize, num_img_embs))\\\\n            att_masks += [0] * num_img_embs\\\\n\\\\n        # Process language tokens\\\\n        def lang_embed_func(tokens):\\\\n            lang_emb = self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(tokens)\\\\n            lang_emb_dim = lang_emb.shape[-1]\\\\n            return lang_emb * math.sqrt(lang_emb_dim)\\\\n\\\\n        lang_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(lang_embed_func, tokens)\\\\n        embs.append(lang_emb)\\\\n        pad_masks.append(masks)\\\\n\\\\n        num_lang_embs = lang_emb.shape[1]\\\\n        att_masks += [0] * num_lang_embs\\\\n\\\\n        embs = torch.cat(embs, dim=1)\\\\n        pad_masks = torch.cat(pad_masks, dim=1)\\\\n        att_masks = torch.tensor(att_masks, dtype=torch.bool, device=pad_masks.device)\\\\n\\\\n        bsize = pad_masks.shape[0]\\\\n        att_masks = att_masks[None, :].expand(bsize, len(att_masks))\\\\n\\\\n        return embs, pad_masks, att_masks\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 6.89}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"d6d82359-e118-47ba-aeae-b71fe82fa077","uuid":"252b2387-9533-404b-b418-0012420dbed2","timestamp":"2026-05-22T12:48:10.228Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"PI05Pytorch has embed_prefix\\\\n    def embed_prefix(\\\\n        self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks\\\\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Embed images with SigLIP and language tokens with embedding layer.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        embs = []\\\\n        pad_masks = []\\\\n        att_masks = []\\\\n\\\\n        # Process images\\\\n        for img, img_mask in zip(images, img_masks, strict=True):\\\\n\\\\n            def image_embed_func(img):\\\\n                return self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_image(img)\\\\n\\\\n            img_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(image_embed_func, img)\\\\n            bsize, num_img_embs = img_emb.shape[:2]\\\\n\\\\n            embs.append(img_emb)\\\\n            pad_masks.append(img_mask[:, None].expand(bsize, num_img_embs))\\\\n            att_masks += [0] * num_img_embs\\\\n\\\\n        # Process language tokens\\\\n        def lang_embed_func(tokens):\\\\n            lang_emb = self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(tokens)\\\\n            lang_emb_dim = lang_emb.shape[-1]\\\\n            return lang_emb * math.sqrt(lang_emb_dim)\\\\n\\\\n        lang_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(lang_embed_func, tokens)\\\\n        embs.append(lang_emb)\\\\n        pad_masks.append(masks)\\\\n\\\\n        num_lang_embs = lang_emb.shape[1]\\\\n        att_masks += [0] * num_lang_embs\\\\n\\\\n        embs = torch.cat(embs, dim=1)\\\\n        pad_masks = torch.cat(pad_masks, dim=1)\\\\n        att_masks = torch.tensor(att_masks, dtype=torch.bool, device=pad_masks.device)\\\\n\\\\n        bsize = pad_masks.shape[0]\\\\n        att_masks = att_masks[None, :].expand(bsize, len(att_masks))\\\\n\\\\n        return embs, pad_masks, att_masks\\\\n\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 6.89}}\"}","structuredContent":{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"PI05Pytorch has embed_prefix\\n    def embed_prefix(\\n        self, images, img_masks, tokens, masks\\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Embed images with SigLIP and language tokens with embedding layer.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        embs = []\\n        pad_masks = []\\n        att_masks = []\\n\\n        # Process images\\n        for img, img_mask in zip(images, img_masks, strict=True):\\n\\n            def image_embed_func(img):\\n                return self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_image(img)\\n\\n            img_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(image_embed_func, img)\\n            bsize, num_img_embs = img_emb.shape[:2]\\n\\n            embs.append(img_emb)\\n            pad_masks.append(img_mask[:, None].expand(bsize, num_img_embs))\\n            att_masks += [0] * num_img_embs\\n\\n        # Process language tokens\\n        def lang_embed_func(tokens):\\n            lang_emb = self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(tokens)\\n            lang_emb_dim = lang_emb.shape[-1]\\n            return lang_emb * math.sqrt(lang_emb_dim)\\n\\n        lang_emb = self._apply_checkpoint(lang_embed_func, tokens)\\n        embs.append(lang_emb)\\n        pad_masks.append(masks)\\n\\n        num_lang_embs = lang_emb.shape[1]\\n        att_masks += [0] * num_lang_embs\\n\\n        embs = torch.cat(embs, dim=1)\\n        pad_masks = torch.cat(pad_masks, dim=1)\\n        att_masks = torch.tensor(att_masks, dtype=torch.bool, device=pad_masks.device)\\n\\n        bsize = pad_masks.shape[0]\\n        att_masks = att_masks[None, :].expand(bsize, len(att_masks))\\n\\n        return embs, pad_masks, att_masks\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 6.89}}"}}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01RgwNcve7rJWcRrKLGP6fC6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Now I have both:\n\n1. `embed_image`: calls `get_image_features`, uses `pooler_output * sqrt(hidden_size)`. \n   - The patched version calls `vision_tower` + `multi_modal_projector` directly, which is functionally equivalent to `get_image_features` (which also does vision_tower + multi_modal_projector and stores result as pooler_output).\n\n2. `embed_language_tokens`: just calls `embed_tokens(tokens)` — raw embeddings, no scaling.\n\nNow in the default `embed_prefix`:\n```python\n\n\nlang_emb = self.paligemma_with_expert.embed_language_tokens(tokens)\nlang_emb_dim = lang_emb.shape[-1]\nreturn lang_emb * math.sqrt(lang_emb_dim)\n```\n\nSo the language embeddings get scaled by the square root of their dimension — for Gemma 2B that's around 45x. But the custom `pruned_embed_prefix` skips this scaling entirely and returns raw embeddings, which is a significant mismatch if the model was trained with this scaling applied.\n\nYet the seed43 results show the unscaled version works perfectly at 99.4% success rate, suggesting the model might have been trained with a different LeRobot version that either didn't include this scaling or had it baked into the embedding layer itself. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01Fmc6eCayPQbdjNg3wbifVx","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"**FOUND IT!**\n\n`GemmaTextScaledWordEmbedding` already applies `* self.embed_scale` in its forward method! And `embed_scale` is set during initialization.\n\nThe default `embed_prefix` in LeRobot ALSO applies `lang_emb * math.sqrt(lang_emb_dim)`. This means language embeddings are being scaled TWICE:\n1. Once by `GemmaTextScaledWordEmbedding.forward()` → `* embed_scale` \n2. Once by `embed_prefix` → `* sqrt(hidden_size)`\n   \nIf `embed_scale = sqrt(hidden_size)`, that's a double-scaling by `sqrt(2048) ≈ 45.25`, which would massively amplify the language embeddings and break the model. But the custom `pruned_embed_prefix` skips that second scaling, so it only gets the one from `embed_tokens`—which is why the pruned version works.
